# UIUC Knowledge Hub — Source Manifest V2

## Purpose

Governed source registry for the UIUC Knowledge Hub RAG. This version adds high-value source identity, retrieval, temporal, crawl, and indexing metadata while retaining the corrected HTML-only corpus.

## Source governance

- **HTML/web only:** PDFs are excluded.
- **Primary sources:** official UIUC/U. Illinois websites.
- **Authorized external:** MTD is explicitly marked `authorized_external` for transportation information.
- **Stable identity:** `source_id` is deterministic from `canonical_url`.
- **Retrieval:** `authority_score`, `retrieval_priority`, `source_role`, `topic`, `subtopic`, and temporal fields support hybrid retrieval.
- **Freshness:** `crawl_frequency`, `last_crawled`, `content_hash`, and `content_changed` support incremental recrawling.
- **Index lifecycle:** `index_status` and `embedding_version` support controlled re-indexing.

## Field definitions

| Field | Purpose |
|---|---|
| `source_id` | Stable source identifier for database relationships and audit trails |
| `canonical_url` | Normalized source identity used for deduplication |
| `source_type` | `uiuc_official` or `authorized_external` |
| `source_role` | Policy, deadline, procedure, course, program, service, etc. |
| `authority_score` | Source authority from 1-10 |
| `retrieval_priority` | Initial 0-100 retrieval boost reflecting authority, role, and temporal status |
| `temporal_scope` | Current, historical, archive, or unspecified |
| `academic_year` | Detected academic year where available |
| `crawl_frequency` | Recommended refresh cadence |
| `last_crawled` | Runtime crawl timestamp to populate |
| `content_hash` | Hash of cleaned page content for change detection |
| `http_status` | HTTP response status from latest crawl |
| `word_count` | Cleaned content size from latest crawl |
| `chunks` | Expected/current chunk count |
| `index_status` | `approved`, `review`, `blocked`, or `deprecated` |
| `embedding_version` | Embedding/index version used for chunks |
| `content_changed` | Whether latest crawl differs from stored content |

> ---
> **NOTE — full inventory, verbatim from the source document supplied 2026-08-18.** This file was
> previously flagged as a 39-row partial sample; it now holds the complete ~1,069-row inventory
> across all 21 topics. A handful of titles below retain mojibake artifacts (`â` in place of an
> en dash or curly apostrophe) inherited from the source document's own encoding — left as-is
> rather than silently "corrected" by guessing the original character, since this file's purpose
> is a faithful record of what was supplied, not a cleaned-up presentation copy.
